# Specification upgrade TODO

Status: all 85 parent milestones are accepted for the declared spec and React-reference profiles. See the [upgrade plan](spec-upgrade-plan.md), [verification baseline](verification-baseline.md) and [tooling commands](linting-and-formatting.md) for the bounded evidence and reproducible gates.

Use the [renderer audit](renderer-parity-audit.md), [source inventory](renderer-source-inventory.md), and [47-example matrix](spec-example-matrix.md) alongside this list. IDs R01–R65 and C01–C10 refer to audit rows. Implementation scope is all current Svelte families, with shadcn first and React Antd/shadcn as references.

## Progress checkpoint — 2026-11-03

**Parent milestones:** 85/85 accepted. No TODO checkboxes remain open.
P1.10 localization is accepted after the source-wide catalog
inventory, localized Shadcn dialog close labels and focused 4/4 production gate.
P1.14 has **44 accepted diagnostic slices**, including all-family native
`gridColumns` warning recovery (focused layout files **5/7/5**) and three
Web Component event cases (**1/1 per family**), plus UI-schema
rule-reference and splitter/container-option delivery with Shadcn Web
Component recovery evidence, alongside the
independent split-pane warning recovery slice. Native missing-renderer/cell callback
recovery and three Web Component missing-renderer event cases are verified;
the 12-area emitter inventory is accepted. The latest host sweep found and fixed a
missing validator author sink and stale schema-interval warning state in all
three Web Components; their complete diagnostic files pass **19/24/19** and
the rebuilt production host recovery file passes **3/3**.

Stepper 14/14 and Tags Input 6/6 files match their current adapted upstream

hosts are documented formatting and Shadow DOM adapters. The
fresh current-main official registry report matches the previous audit for all

regression in all three Svelte families: replacing a two-key object whose

visible dynamic rows (1/1 focused case per family). The component-local
`PropertyIdentityStore` uses a key map rather than a delimiter-joined
signature. Other literal-key context paths are accepted in later P1.3/P4.3 checkpoints.

P4.3/P5.4 React dynamic-property label parity found a real unrestricted-value
duplicate in all three families. The mixed type selector keeps the visible
property label; the delegated primitive label is now visually hidden only
when it matches the enclosing literal property's label, retaining the input's
accessible name and live locale updates. The two affected component files
pass 47/47 Flowbite, 47/47 shadcn and 49/49 Skeleton; all three Svelte checks
report zero diagnostics. The rebuilt additional-properties and mixed-control
production files pass 136/136 combined family/locale/native/Web Component

### Visible acceptance checkpoints

Top-level status: **85/85 accepted; 0 open**.
The checked steps below record the specific evidence behind each parent acceptance.

**P1.3 literal paths: 18/18 checkpoints accepted**

- [x] Native rules, controls, table cells, OneOf, ListWithDetail, recursive detail, summaries, and rename preserve literal dotted/empty/prototype-looking members (focused native suites cited in P1.3).
- [x] Built-demo literal versus nested control errors separate in the 12/12 family × locale × host matrix; focused NoValidation/ValidateAndHide collision cases pass 2/2 per family.
- [x] Resolve ambiguous dotted `ValidateFunctionContext.path` prefixes in `ruleValidation.ts`: rule adapters now capture exact dispatch members, with optional `RendererProps.pathSegments` for a caller that owns a literal dotted path. The existing light/dark file capture harness also passed its focused Shadcn EN native case; broader locale/theme visual review remains open.

Complete affected File control files pass **50/50 Flowbite, 50/50 Shadcn and 49/49 Skeleton**; base String control files pass **8/8 each**. All affected base/extended/Web Component/demo builds passed for this vertical change. Refreshed dark BG screenshots show full two-line labels clear of their pickers. Other long-label control profiles remain open.
- [x] Horizontal checkbox labels now wrap instead of truncating in all three renderer-owned wrappers. A long BG label at 320 px stays inside the form and still activates its checkbox in **1/1 mounted case per family** and **6/6 rebuilt native/Web Component production cases**. The complete affected Boolean files pass **5/5 each**. All three base/extended/Web Component/demo dependency chains rebuild; the base checks have zero errors/warnings. Other horizontal-label presentations remain in the broad P8.2 review.

Whole-card touch activation switches from Approve to Decline and back, preserving selection, card containment and no horizontal page overflow or page errors. All three Web Components and demos rebuilt after the validator author-sink change; their checks report zero errors, with the known Web Component `customElement` source-check warning. The later schema-interval rebuild passes its focused production diagnostic file **3/3**; other touch profiles remain open.
- [x] The built React and Svelte Shadcn demos now have a bounded side-by-side `spec-choice-controls` card review at 1280 px and 390 px. Both wrap the two cards from one row into a narrow vertical stack without lost labels, image, selected content, selected border, page errors or horizontal overflow. React uses content-width cards; Svelte uses equal-width cards inside the available form width. Four captures and the bounded comparison are recorded in the [side-by-side review](shadcn-side-by-side-review.md); broader themes and fixtures remain open.

Representative Shadcn dark BG focus, Skeleton light BG error, Flowbite dark EN error and Skeleton dark BG focus captures were inspected; focus and error states remain legible and unclipped. The earlier Web Component show-radio/error matrix passes 6/6; broader card contexts and other visual profiles remain open.

Both retain selected-card feedback, images and labels without horizontal overflow or page errors. Captures and the bounded finding are recorded in the [side-by-side review](shadcn-side-by-side-review.md).

This directly verifies the spec §18.21.3 touch-action requirement beyond choice cards.

The popup remains inside the viewport; no page errors occur.

**P8.3 browser performance: all 5 profiles accepted**

- [x] The canonical plain-text control retains focus and the end caret after 35 sequential keys in each Flowbite/Shadcn/Skeleton native and Web Component host, with no browser errors. The six-case browser profile performs one warm-up and five measured edits per host; measured medians are **18.1–20.8 ms** and per-host p95 values are **19.3–23.0 ms** on the local headless Chromium run. All **6/6** cases pass a 500 ms p95 smoke budget for this 35-key edit. This budget is scoped to the test environment and is not a universal device guarantee.
- [x] The canonical recursive-tree demo renders a folder with 100 file children after a real expand action in each native and Web Component host. All **6/6** browser cases pass after one warm-up and five measured expand/collapse cycles per host; medians are **41.4–121.1 ms** and p95 values are **41.6–142.8 ms** against a 1,000 ms per-host smoke budget. Each cycle waits for the last child to become visible, then hidden on collapse, and the page has no browser errors.
- [x] The canonical AG Grid demo accepts 300 applicants and repeatedly filters to the last row, edits its name, clears the filter, and reopens it to verify the committed value. All **6/6** native/Web Component cases pass after one warm-up and five measured cycles per host, with no browser errors. Filter p95 values are **502.0–987.7 ms** against a 2,000 ms per-host smoke budget; edit p95 values are **132.7–1,017.8 ms** against a 3,000 ms budget. Skeleton's native filter is the slowest observed host, so the budget includes it rather than assuming Web Component/native parity from the faster families.
- [x] The canonical oneOf contact editor remains responsive with `validateActiveBranch` off and on: after one warm-up and five measured edits per mode, all **6/6** family × host cases preserve the contact name, focus and end caret with no browser errors. Inactive p95 values are **21.0–26.4 ms** and active p95 values are **19.3–40.0 ms**, both under a 500 ms per-host smoke budget. The separate 40-field helper sample verifies compiled-validator identity reuse and zero inactive activation; the browser profile measures the actual smaller canonical composition.
- [x] SPA navigation away from the AG Grid and Monaco Data editor detaches each old surface in all six family × host scenarios, then remounts the requested host. One warm-up and five measured route cycles per host pass **6/6** with no page errors. Grid teardown p95 is **75.9–91.7 ms**, grid remount **325.1–524.2 ms**, Monaco teardown **85.4–93.5 ms**, and Monaco remount **298.9–627.9 ms**, each under a 3,000 ms per-host smoke budget. Shadcn and Skeleton retain hidden grid tab content, so the test uses their app navigation boundary to measure real teardown.

### Execution order and stop conditions

The canonical tuple's empty-order-line initialization and numeric/string draft
semantics now pass a 12/12 built-demo matrix across families, EN/BG and both
hosts; see the [tuple position draft checkpoint](verification-baseline.md#p49-tuple-position-drafts-in-production-2026-10-31).
The complete P4.9 functional profile is accepted; cross-family visual review remains under P8.2.

The dynamic-property action catalog now passes 160/160 rebuilt production
cases across the affected AdditionalProperties, minimum-constraint and
pagination files; see the [action catalog checkpoint](verification-baseline.md#dynamic-property-action-catalog--2026-11-01).
Shadcn's selected-row confirmation now has the React portable title, message
and action in EN/BG with focused native coverage. The rebuilt collection
pagination production matrix passes 12/12 across families, locales and hosts.
OneOf branch-clear actions now use the React portable `oneOf.clear` key in
all three families; the full native OneOfRenderer files pass 17/17 Flowbite,
17/17 Shadcn and 19/19 Skeleton. The rebuilt recursive-oneOf clear-slot
production matrix passes 12/12. UI-schema registry cycle fallback now emits a
coded author warning and detects fresh equivalent entries; 4/4 focused cases
pass per family, plus fresh-object and valid registered-detail cases in Shadcn. The broader P1.14
diagnostic channel remains open. The Svelte-only global minimum item width uses `config.jsonformsSvelte.layoutDefaults`; focused shared and native layout suites pass.
Literal dotted-parent ListWithDetail edits now have focused native acceptance in all three families, preserving the nested lookalike and routing errors to the literal array. Other P1.2/P1.3 profiles remain open.
Authored tuple layouts now have focused mounted acceptance for undeclared position scopes and type-less detail fallback in all three families (6/6). The invalid scope reports the diagnostic required by spec §18.23 without inventing a field or changing data; the full P4.9 functional profile is now accepted.
The tuple scope diagnostic now also reaches authors as a deduplicated `tuple.invalidScope` warning with path and scope, separate from form validation; the accessible explanation remains for the missing position.
ImageView diagnostics now reach authors with stable code, severity, message, path and scope, once per mounted condition and without leaking the URL; a focused mounted Shadcn case passes. Accessible warnings remain for unavailable image content; missing alt text now reaches authors without an in-form warning when the image renders. Other P1.14 diagnostic surfaces remain open.
The imported invalid Fax choice pill now has 12/12 built-demo border checks across family, locale and native/Web Component hosts, including all four sides and the transparent valid sibling. Six narrow scrolling-container screenshots in English were inspected across all families and both hosts; other themes/locales and components remain under P8.2.
Markdown URL substitutions now encode parentheses as URL components so unmatched delimiters cannot end the authored link. The focused interpolation suite passes 15/15, and a fresh demo build plus 12/12 production scenarios verify URL encoding and live host-data replacement across families, EN/BG and both hosts. Other P1.9 URL consumers remain open.
Refused Markdown links and disabled/unsupported markup now also emit deduplicated `markup.*` author warnings without logging URL values. The full mounted MarkupLabelRenderer file passes 10/10; the visible readable fallback remains. The broader P1.14 diagnostic channel and other policy surfaces remain open.
An unmatched initial category now emits a structured `categorization.initialNotFound` author warning with severity and `details.initial`; the first-visible fallback and form validity are unchanged. The focused Shadcn category suite passes 4/4 against the rebuilt base package. The broader diagnostic channel remains open.
An unhandled extended form action now emits a structured `action.unhandled` author warning with action/element details, still once per action and without adding a form error; the focused host suite passes 6/6.
Named template diagnostics now include severity, stable message, path and name details. The dispatch cycle guard distinguishes different named templates at one data path, fixing nested template rendering while still stopping true cycles and fresh-equivalent Control self-dispatch; focused template and registry suites pass 21/21 and 6/6. The broader template policy/host matrix and diagnostic channel remain open.
Shared missing-renderer/cell, UI-schema cycle and tuple author warnings now carry severity and stable messages in addition to their existing code and context. Base JsonForms passes 18/18 and Shadcn tuple passes 51/51 after a base rebuild. Remaining P1.14 emitters and host delivery remain open.
The active-request `focus.conflict` warning now carries severity, stable message and `details.paths`, without selecting a focus winner. The shared suite passes 2/2 and the two focused native files pass 13/13 per family after a base rebuild. Specialized focusability and the broader diagnostic channel remain open.

When no primitive label exists, array and ListWithDetail views use a localized item number. Unhandled-action diagnostics now reset per action after a trusted host handler processes it, so removing the handler later reports the unhandled action again. The extended host callback case passes the absent/present/absent handler sequence, and the extended Svelte check is clean.
The Shadcn Web Component now passes a focused live unsupported/valid/unsupported UI-schema transition with two composed author events and unchanged form data. This verifies the package boundary preserves the mounted diagnostic reset; other host event profiles remain open under P1.14.
ListWithDetail reorder now has focused native identity evidence in all three families (3/3): the selected detail input stays mounted, follows its row key to the new index, and its next edit changes that row only. Mounted tuple details in all three native families create an own `constructor` field from an absent value and commit exactly that member (3/3 focused); other literal/prototype-looking tuple paths remain for the broader P1.3 audit.
Boolean/slider array-parent checks and composite-cell parent removal policy now use the same core own-data resolver. Three focused native cases pass in each family (9/9 total) after a base rebuild. Broader literal-path acceptance remains before P1.3 can close.
Composite-cell parent schema traversal now checks own declared members before `additionalProperties`, so `constructor`, `__proto__` and `toString` do not resolve through inherited prototype members. Its focused helper case passes (1/1); the base build/check/publint gate is clean, and mounted minProperties composite cases pass in all three native families (3/3) against that build.

Control grid options, portable columns and cell presentation are reviewed. Continue with shared precedence decisions (P1.2).**
   **Regression checkpoint:** the reported integration failures and invalid
   dynamic-key presentation are resolved. The shadcn-only package stages and
   580-case production matrix pass. That checkpoint preceded the completed **177/177**
   local-option count; see [verification-baseline.md](verification-baseline.md).
3. **Other shared foundations still open:** finish config
   consumption/precedence, then presentation/executable policy. Error routing
   and validator acceptance above are complete; preserve their passing evidence.
   Finish a specific prerequisite before resuming any dependent renderer batch.
4. **Remaining renderer contracts:** close each ledger row's missing behavior and
   acceptance, using existing implementations and reference scenarios before adding
   adapters or tests. Resolve shared prerequisites before dependent UI work.
5. **Host/package and cross-cutting acceptance:** finish residual demo/Web Component,
   minimum-version/packed-consumer, accessibility and performance checks. Upstream UI
   review is required before importing upstream changes, not before unrelated fixes.
6. **Release gate:** reconcile all evidence and capabilities, finish migration docs,
   then run final full checks. Required implementation gaps cannot be deferred into
   a claim of release readiness.

For every batch, report: **requirement closed; implementation changed; evidence;
remaining blocker; next batch**. Update this checkpoint and the owning P-item together.
Reopen accepted behavior only with a concrete failing case, changed requirement or
changed dependency, and record that reason. Use focused checks during implementation;
rebuild dependent artifacts before browser checks, never concurrently with tests
that consume them. Broad final checks belong at integration/release checkpoints.

### Why elapsed work exceeds visible checkbox progress

The existing Svelte renderers are the starting implementation, not a rewrite target.
This scope also includes three native UI families, two host modes, locales, demos,
packaging, CI/tooling and upstream component review. Those are separate deliverables
from renderer behavior and should not obscure it. The records also show avoidable
cost: broad parent items remained open after smaller behaviors were accepted,
progress was scattered across documents, and browser fixture/selector corrections
and overlapping builds required reruns. The execution rules above address that cost.
There is no comparable measured React timeline/effort audit here, so this document
does not attribute the difference to Svelte complexity or claim React received less
validation. Remaining effort cannot be inferred from checkbox or test counts; estimate
each bounded batch after identifying its actual missing scenarios.

## Native component selection

Before implementing or revising a renderer, inspect the installed target library's
exports/source and official component documentation. Prefer its composed component
before building UI from primitives. Record a concrete capability limitation for
custom behavior. Preserve the explicitly requested compact File presentation.
Shadcn OTP now uses the official Input OTP Root/Group/Slot components; the earlier
Bits UI primitive adapter has been replaced.

## Completion rule

Each item requires source/spec references, implementation, relevant executed tests, English and Bulgarian text, demo coverage, and an updated capability result. Record command/result and reviewed UI evidence in the implementing change. A documented required gap remains open. Tests must exercise public registries and exact stored values; preserve invalid fixtures and unchanged siblings. Follow the spec when the React implementation differs.

Component hosts now checks root-level rules on literal dotted, empty and
repeatedly escaped tilde keys. Live edits hide and restore each rule target;
nested and escaped lookalikes do not control them. The same matrix now checks
literal dotted rule scopes inside two array items: editing one item's nested
lookalike leaves its target visible, editing its literal key hides that target,
and expanding/editing the other item governs only its own target. A host data
replacement that swaps the rows preserves that rule-to-item relationship in
all 12 cases. Other arbitrary dotted instance-path consumers still need
review.

Recursive-tree literal child paths now also pass a 12/12 built-demo matrix
across Shadcn, Flowbite, Skeleton, EN/BG and native/Web Component hosts. The
matrix selects a child and grandchild through a literal dotted `childrenProperty`,
follows the detail link, edits the grandchild, and confirms that the nested
lookalike remains unchanged in host JSON. The demo editor can replace a mounted
recursive-tree UI schema without throwing while the old renderer unmounts.

Color and duration edits after sorting update the correct source item; invalid typed drafts remain local to the focused row, and valid edits move the item to its new sort position. Null cells toggle the sorted source row between null and absence without changing siblings or the original input. For integer cells, an edit after sorting updates the correct source item, shows an error only in its row, retains focus, and a correction clears the error while moving that item to its new sort position. A focused AG Grid date/date-time matrix passes 2/2 per extended family: after sorting, invalid and corrected edits update the original source row, attach feedback to that row, preserve input focus during the invalid edit, and move the corrected row to its new sorted position. JSON-authored schemas cannot carry callback functions; this runtime compatibility surface does not change the portable string-format contract. ## Deferred designs and separate work

- Dynamic UI overlays, remote-choice APIs, speculative new clear/remove option names, and timezone-selector proposals remain governed by the spec backlog; do not publish new authoring shapes in this upgrade by inference.
- A graphical schema/UI editor is outside this runtime upgrade. Preserve unknown authoring data and support the runtime conditional-field contract; do not recreate the editor removed from this project without a separate request.
- React's remaining defects are evidence for additional acceptance tests, not permission to implement the same defect in Svelte.

example matrix describe remaining behavioral coverage. Historical fixture counts
and intermediate failures are not current completion status.
